* given byte.\n697\t\t *\n698\t\t * @param data Bytes to convert to hexadecimal characters.\n699\t\t * @return A string containing hexadecimal characters.\n700\t\t */\n701\t\tpublic static String image\n1269\t\t\tfinal int height = options.outHeight;\n1270\t\t\tfinal int width = options.outWidth;\n1271\t\t\tint inSampleSize = 1;\n1272\t\n1273\t\t\tif (height > reqHeight || width > reqWidth)\n1274\t\t\t{\n1275\t\n1276\t\t\t\t// Calculate ratios of height and width to requested height and\n1277\t\t\t\t// width\n1278\t\t\t\tfinal int heightRatio = Math.round((float) height / (float) reqHeight);\n1279\t\t\t\tfinal int widthRatio = Math.round((float) width / (float) reqWidth);\n1280\t\n1281\t\t\t\t// Choose the smallest ratio as inSampleSize value, this will\n1282\t\t\t\t// guarantee\n1283\t\t\t\t// a final image with both dimensions larger than or equal to the\n1284\t\t\t\t// requested height and width.\n1285\t\t\t\tinSampleSize = heightRatio < widthRatio ? heightRatio method reads credentials from SharedPreferences:\n\n```java\npublic static String getRestUrl(Context context, String method) {\n    StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder(8192);\n    \n    SharedPreferences preferences = getPreferences(context);\n    \n    int instance = preferences.getInt(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_SERVER_INSTANCE, 1);\n    String serverUrl = preferences.getString(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_SERVER_URL + instance, null);\n    String username = preferences.getString(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_USERNAME + instance, null);\n    String password = preferences.getString(Constants.PREFERENCES_KEY_PASSWORD + instance, null);\n    \n    // Slightly obfuscate password\n    password = "enc:" + Util.utf8HexEncode(password);\n    \n    builder.append(serverUrl);\n    if (builder.charAt(builder.length() - 1) != \'/\')\n    {\n        builder.append(\'/\');\n    }\n    \n    builder.append("rest/").append(method).append(".view");\n    builder.append("?u=").append(username);\n    builder.append("&p=").append(password);\n    builder.append("&v=").append(Constants.REST_PROTOCOL_VERSION);\n    builder.append("&c=").append(Constants.REST_CLIENT_ID);\n    \n    return builder.toString();\n}\n```\n\n**Key Helper Method: getPreferences() - Lines 401-404**\n\n```java\npublic static 0;\n\t\tint n;\n\n\t\twhile (-1 != (n = input.read(buffer)))\n\t\t{\n\t\t\toutput.write(buffer, 0, n);\n\t\t\tcount += n;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn count;\n\t}\n\n\tpublic static void atomicCopy(File from, File to) throws IOException\n\t{\n\t\tFile tmp = new File(String.format("%s.tmp", to.getPath()));\n\t\tFileInputStream in = new FileInputStream(from);\n\t\tFileOutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(tmp);\n\n\t\ttry\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tin.getChannel().transferTo(0, from.length(), out.getChannel());\n\t\t\tout.close();\n\n\t\t\tif (!tmp.renameTo(to))\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tthrow new IOException(String.format("Failed to rename %s to %s", tmp, to));\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tLog.i(TAG, String.format("Copied %s to %s", from, to));\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (IOException x)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tclose(out);\n\t\t\tdelete(to);\n\t\t\tthrow x;\n\t\t}\n\t\tfinally\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tclose(in);\n\t\t\tclose(out);\n\t\t\tdelete(tmp);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpublic static void renameFile(File * <li><code>format(918)</code> returns <em>"918 B"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(98765)</code> returns <em>"96 KB"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(1238476)</code> returns <em>"1.2 MB"</em>.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t * This method assumes that 1 KB is 1024 bytes.\n\t * To get a localized string, please use formatLocalizedBytes instead.\n\t *\n\t * @param byteCount The number of bytes.\n\t * @return The formatted string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static synchronized String formatBytes(long byteCount)\n\t{\n\n\t\t// More than 1 GB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tNumberFormat gigaByteFormat = GIGA_BYTE_FORMAT;\n\t\t\treturn gigaByteFormat.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 MB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tNumberFormat megaByteFormat = MEGA_BYTE_FORMAT;\n\t\t\treturn megaByteFormat.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 KB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tNumberFormat kiloByteFormat = KILO_BYTE_FORMAT;\n\t\t\treturn kiloByteFormat.format((double) byteCount / 1024);\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn byteCount + " B";\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Converts a byte-count to a formatted string suitable for display to the user.\n\t * For instance:\n\t * <ul>\n\t * <li><code>format(918)</code> returns <em>"918 B"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(98765)</code> returns <em>"96 KB"</em>.</li>\n\t * <li><code>format(1238476)</code> returns <em>"1.2 MB"</em>.</li>\n\t * </ul>\n\t * This method assumes that 1 KB is 1024 bytes.\n\t * This version of the method returns a localized string.\n\t *\n\t * @param byteCount The number of bytes.\n\t * @return The formatted string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static synchronized String formatLocalizedBytes(long byteCount, Context context)\n\t{\n\n\t\t// More than 1 GB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (GIGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == GIGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 MB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024 * 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (MEGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == MEGA_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT.format((double) byteCount / (1024 * 1024));\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// More than 1 KB?\n\t\tif (byteCount >= 1024)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tif (KILO_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT == null)\n\t\t\t{\n\t\t\t\tKILO_BYTE_LOCALIZED_FORMAT = new DecimalFormat(context.getResources().getString(R.string.util_bytes_format_kilobyte));\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\treturn representation of its UTF-8 bytes.\n\t *\n\t * @param s The string to encode.\n\t * @return The encoded string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static String utf8HexEncode(String s)\n\t{\n\t\tif (s == null)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tbyte[] utf8;\n\n\t\ttry\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tutf8 = s.getBytes(Constants.UTF_8);\n\t\t}\n\t\tcatch (UnsupportedEncodingException x)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tthrow new The returned array will be double the length of the passed array, as it takes two characters to represent any\n\t * given byte.\n\t *\n\t * @param data Bytes to convert to hexadecimal characters.\n\t * @return A string containing hexadecimal characters.\n\t */\n\tpublic static String hexEncode(byte[] data)\n\t{\n\t\tint length = data.length;\n\t\tchar[] out = new char[length << 1];\n\t\tint j = 0;\n\n\t\t// two characters form the hex value.\n\t\tfor (byte aData : data)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tout[j++] = HEX_DIGITS[(0xF0 & aData) >>> 4];\n\t\t\tout[j++] = HEX_DIGITS[0x0F & aData];\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn new String(out);\n\t}\n\n\t/**\n\t * Calculates the MD5 digest and returns the value as a 32 character hex string.\n\t *\n\t * @param s Data to digest.\n\t * @return MD5 digest as a hex string.\n\t */\n\tpublic static String md5Hex(String s)\n\t{\n\t\tif (s == null)\n\t\t{\n\t\t\treturn null;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttry\n\t\t{\n\t\t\tMessageDigest md5 = MessageDigest.getInstance("MD5");\n\t\t\treturn reqWidth, int reqHeight)\n\t{\n\t\t// Raw height and width of image\n\t\tfinal int height = options.outHeight;\n\t\tfinal int width = options.outWidth;\n\t\tint inSampleSize = 1;\n\n\t\tif (height > reqHeight || width > reqWidth)\n\t\t{\n\n\t\t\t// Calculate ratios of height and width to requested height and\n\t\t\t// width\n\t\t\tfinal int heightRatio = Math.round((float) height / (float) reqHeight);\n\t\t\tfinal int widthRatio = Math.round((float) width / (float) reqWidth);\n\n\t\t\t// Choose the smallest ratio as inSampleSize value, this will\n\t\t\t// guarantee\n\t\t\t// a final image with both dimensions larger than or equal to the\n\t\t\t// requested height and width.\n\t\t\tinSampleSize = heightRatio < widthRatio ? heightRatio : widthRatio;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treturn inSampleSize;\n\t}\n\n\tpublic static void linkButtons(Context context, RemoteViews views, boolean playerActive)\n\t{\n\n\t\tIntent intent = new Intent(context, playerActive ? AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The fix is complete and the project builds successfully.\n\n## Root cause\n\nThe app was writing settings to one `SharedPreferences` file and reading them from a **different** one.\n\n- Everything in the app reads settings via `Util.getPreferences()` → `context.getSharedPreferences("org.moire.ultrasonic_preferences", 0)` (`Constants.PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME`).\n- But `ServerSettingsFragment` (and `SettingsFragment`) used `PreferenceManager.getDefaultSharedPreferences()`, which resolves to `"<applicationId>_preferences"`. `ultrasonic/build.gradle` sets `applicationIdSuffix ".debug"`, so on a debug build that is **`"org.moire.ultrasonic.debug_preferences"`** — a completely separate file. The `PreferenceFragment` itself also persisted its preference widgets there, since `addPreferencesFromResource()` uses the fragment\'s own `PreferenceManager`.\n\nSo the server URL/username/password the user typed landed in `org.moire.ultrasonic.debug_preferences`, while `MusicServiceFactory.createSubsonicApiClient()` looked in `org.moire.ultrasonic_preferences`, found `serverUrl == null`, and hit this fallback:\n\n```java\nLog.i("MusicServiceFactory", "Server credentials is not available");\nreturn new SubsonicAPIClient("http://localhost", "", "", ...);\n```\n\nThat is exactly the reported `Failed to connect to localhost/127.0.0.1:80`. Two details in the report confirm it: the logcat process is `org.moire.ultrasonic.debug` (a debug build, where the two names diverge), and "works fine with 2.2.2" — in a *release* build the default name coincidentally equals `PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME`, so the bug is invisible there.\n\n## Changes\n\n1. **`ServerSettingsFragment.java`** — point the fragment\'s `PreferenceManager` at `Constants.PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME` *before* `addPreferencesFromResource()`, and read via `Util.getPreferences()`.\n2. **`SettingsFragment.java`** — same fix; it had the identical defect for all the main app settings.\n3. **`Util.getActiveServers()`** — was the one remaining reader using default prefs, so the server count was read from the wrong file too; switched to `getPreferences(context)`.\n4. **`ServerSettingsFragment`** — added `invalidateCachedMusicService()`, called when the active server\'s URL/username/password/SSL/LDAP settings change. `MusicServiceFactory` caches the `SubsonicAPIClient` in a static, so without this an edit to the active server would still be served by a stale client until something else called `Util.setActiveServer()`.\n\nI verified no `getDefaultSharedPreferences` call sites remain in `ultrasonic/src/`, and `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` reports `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.\n\nOne note worth flagging: this fix changes which file settings are stored in, so on a debug build any server already configured under the old `*.debug_preferences` file will appear reset and need re-entering. Release builds are unaffected, since their default name already matched. Two details in the report confirm it: the logcat process is `org.moire.ultrasonic.debug` (a debug build, where the two names diverge), and "works fine with 2.2.2" — in a *release* build the default name coincidentally equals `PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME`, so the bug is invisible there.\n\n## Changes\n\n1. **`ServerSettingsFragment.java`** — point the fragment\'s `PreferenceManager` at `Constants.PREFERENCES_FILE_NAME` *before* `addPreferencesFromResource()`, and read via `Util.getPreferences()`.\n2. **`SettingsFragment.java`** — same fix; it had the identical defect for all the main app settings.\n3. **`Util.getActiveServers()`** — was the one remaining reader using default prefs, so the server count was read from the wrong file too; switched to `getPreferences(context)`.\n4. **`ServerSettingsFragment`** — added `invalidateCachedMusicService()`, called when the active server\'s URL/username/password/SSL/LDAP settings change. `MusicServiceFactory` caches the `SubsonicAPIClient` in a static, so without this an edit to the active server would still be served by a stale client until something else called `Util.setActiveServer()`.\n\nI verified no `getDefaultSharedPreferences` call sites remain in `ultrasonic/src/`, and `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` reports `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.\n\nOne note worth flagging: this fix changes which file settings are stored in, so on a debug build any server already configured under the old `*.debug_preferences` file will appear reset and need re-entering. Release builds are unaffected, since their default name already matched.
